Combined with local zooming, it reaches 69.6% and 64.4%, but does not close the gap. The GlassGround dataset and code are available at https://github.com/yunnw/GlassGround.

</details>


### [69] [ScanAR: Parallel Row-Scan Decoding for Efficient Image Generation in Unified Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33761)
*Jingen Qu,Xi Chen,Yingbo Tang,Xinghang Li,Tao Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScanAR 仅重定义视觉 token 的前驱依赖，采用"首行逐 token + 后续每行单步并行"的行扫描并行解码，将统一多模态自回归模型的视觉解码深度从 HW 降至 H+W，在不改动骨干、词表与输出头、不增加参数的前提下实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态 AR 模型以单一 token 序列和共享的下一 token 预测头生成所有模态，但其图像生成按光栅顺序逐 token 解码 H×W 网格，需 HW 个串行步，成为推理时间瓶颈。已有视觉 AR 加速方法要么改动 tokenizer、预测头或模型接口，要么保留模型却难以在保质量的前提下实现高并行，因而无法迁移到统一 AR 模型。

Method: 提出 ScanAR 行扫描并行解码：第一视觉行照常从左到右生成；此后每一行在单个并行步内生成，每个 token 由上一行垂直对齐位置的 token 预测；非视觉 token 保持标准自回归规则。该方法只重定义视觉 token 的前驱，视觉解码深度由 HW 降为 H+W，不需修改骨干网络、词表或输出头，也不新增参数，并同时支持从头训练与轻量后训练。

Result: 在 Emu3.5-Image-34B 上，后训练版 ScanAR 达到 GenEval 82.36，为同数据训练的后训练加速方法中最佳，相同推理设置下 512×512 图像生成比光栅解码快 13.8×；在 ImageNet 上，从头训练的 ScanAR 在各模型规模下吞吐均最高，FID 与光栅 LlamaGen 相当；在 34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 上保持了具身场景生成、图文交错生成与视频生成的总体质量。

Conclusion: ScanAR 表明，仅通过重构视觉 token 的条件依赖即可在统一多模态 AR 框架内获得大幅并行加速，且质量损失可忽略；方法通用性强，可无缝嵌入现有统一 AR 模型并适用于多种生成任务。

Abstract: Unified multimodal autoregressive (AR) models represent text and images in one token sequence and generate all modalities with a shared next-token prediction head. Their image generation, however, decodes an $H\times W$ visual grid token by token in raster order, which takes $HW$ sequential steps and dominates inference time. Existing acceleration methods for visual AR generation either change the tokenizer, the prediction head, or the model interface, or keep the model but cannot reach high parallelism without losing quality, so they do not carry over to unified AR models. We propose ScanAR, a row-scan parallel decoding method that only redefines the predecessors of visual tokens. The first visual row is generated from left to right as usual, and each later row is generated in a single parallel step, with every token predicted from the vertically aligned token of the previous row. Nonvisual tokens keep the standard autoregressive rule. ScanAR reduces the visual decoding depth from $HW$ to $H+W$ without changing the backbone, vocabulary, or output head and without adding parameters, and it supports both training from scratch and lightweight post-training. On Emu3.5-Image-34B, ScanAR post-training reaches a GenEval score of 82.36, the best among post-training acceleration methods trained on the same data, and generates $512\times512$ images $13.8\times$ faster than raster decoding under the same inference setting. On ImageNet, ScanAR trained from scratch has the highest throughput at every model size with FID comparable to raster LlamaGen. On the 34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 model, ScanAR preserves the overall quality of embodied scene generation, interleaved image-text generation, and video generation.

</details>


### [70] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Immediate and Prospective Credit Assignment for Medical Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33777)
*Yuqi Cheng,Rui Meng,Yijin Li,Chandra Sekhar Reddy Edula,Xiuwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPACT：面向医学VQA的智能体强化学习框架，通过"即时答案支撑"与"前瞻性引导"两类观测信用，在GRPO中对工具调用token做细粒度信用分配，提升准确率、OOD泛化并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA需要获取有用证据并决定下一步观察位置，但仅用最终答案奖励无法揭示哪些观测支撑了回答，也无法引导后续检查，导致观测策略缺乏可解释与高效的信用信号。

Method: 提出选择性观察策略的智能体RL框架。核心是内容受控估计器：在共享真实观测历史下，比较由真实观测与匹配的替代观测所诱导的下一动作差异，从而隔离观测内容对后续决策的贡献；将即时答案支撑与前瞻引导两类信用结合进GRPO，把观测信用分配给对应工具调用token，并叠加轨迹级结果监督。单一视觉语言模型，图像-问题-答案监督，信用估计仅用于训练阶段。

Result: 在4B与8B骨干、六个医学VQA基准上，准确率提升，OOD泛化强，推理成本显著降低；案例显示模型避免重复观测，并利用已获取证据指导后续检查。

Conclusion: 细粒度、内容受控的观测信用可有效塑造医学VQA智能体的观察策略，在性能、泛化与效率间取得良好平衡，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (VQA) requires acquiring useful evidence and deciding where to look next. Yet final-answer rewards do not reveal which observations support answering or guide subsequent inspection. We introduce MedPACT, an agentic reinforcement learning framework that learns selective observation policies through immediate answer support and prospective guidance. Our content-controlled estimator compares next actions induced by real and matched alternative observations under a shared real-observation history, isolating how observed content improves subsequent decisions. We combine both forms of credit within GRPO, assigning observation credit to the corresponding tool-call tokens alongside trajectory-level outcome supervision. MedPACT uses a single vision-language model and image-question-answer supervision, with credit estimation confined to training. Experiments with 4B and 8B backbones on six medical VQA benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy, strong out-of-distribution generalization, and substantially lower inference costs. Case studies further illustrate how MedPACT avoids repeated observations and uses acquired evidence to guide subsequent inspection, demonstrating real-world practice potential.

</details>


### [71] [DuoWM:Integrating Dual 6-DoF Visual Flows into Action-Conditioned Drone World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33779)
*Zixuan Li,Kangyao Huang,Meng Xu,Wan Tian,Xiaoyu Tang,Jinge Sun,Yingliang Yao,He Chen,Lingzhong Meng,Yunzhi Xue,Jingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoWM—一种双视觉条件的无人机视频世界模型，通过 Action flow 与 Object flow 对齐相机指令并建模 6-DoF 场景变换，在视频质量、3D 一致性、轨迹跟随上均达最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型难以将相机指令与视觉动态对齐，且无法表示完整 6-DoF 场景变换。

Method: 提出 DuoWM，采用两种互补视觉条件：Action flow 将未来相机位置与朝向投影到目标帧；Object flow 对初始观测做前向变形并叠加局部 6-DoF 变换以锚定场景运动。另引入变换感知预训练，在标准 RGB 视频训练前将物体外观与显式变换轴配对。

Result: 在 Sekai-drone 数据集上于视频质量、3D 一致性与轨迹跟随三项指标最优；相较 SOTA 方法 Captain Safari，真实域 FVD 与 LPIPS 分别降低 17.2% 与 21.6%，游戏域 AUC@30 由 0.5003 提升至 0.5719，AUC@15 由 0.3088 提升至 0.3747。

Conclusion: 双流条件与变换感知预训练可有效提升动作条件世界模型的相机-视觉对齐能力与 6-DoF 场景变换建模质量。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models generate future observations from an initial frame and a camera trajectory, but often fail to align camera commands with visual dynamics or represent full 6-DoF scene transformations. We present DuoWM, a first-person drone video world model with two complementary visual conditions. Action flow projects future camera positions and orientations into each target frame, while Object flow forward-warps the initial observation and overlays local 6-DoF transformations to ground the induced scene motion. We further introduce transform-aware pretraining, which pairs object appearance with explicit transformation axes before standard RGB video training. On Sekai-drone dataset, DuoWM achieves the best results across video quality, 3D consistency, and trajectory following. Compared with Captain Safari, the state-of-the-art method, our approach reduces real-domain FVD and LPIPS by 17.2\% and 21.6\%, respectively, while improving game-domain AUC@30 from 0.5003 to 0.5719 and AUC@15 from 0.3088 to 0.3747.

</details>


### [72] [Bridging Transferability and Discriminability via Positive-Unlabeled Domain Adaptation for Building Damage Mapping Across Disaster Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33790)
*Qirui Wang,Hengwei Zhao,Xiaoshu Yang,Jingtao Li,Bo Li,Sorin Popescu,Rusty Feagin,Wenzhe Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正样本锚定的无监督域适应范式PANDA，用于跨事件建筑损毁制图，仅需目标域少量标注损毁建筑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损毁制图对应急响应至关重要，但灾种、地理环境、建筑特征与成像条件差异导致跨事件分布偏移，即便地理空间基础模型（GFM）也难以泛化；而UDA在损毁比例与损毁外观偏移共存且交互时仍具挑战。

Method: 识别出提升目标域判别性是关键，受正样本-无标注（PU）学习启发，提出PU域适应范式，引入PU弱监督（仅需目标域少量标注损毁建筑）；据此构建PANDA，含两个互补互促模块：Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness（PAIR）用于判别性表征学习，Refine Gate and Verifier（RAV）用于监督精炼。

Result: 在全球尺度多灾种基准上总体优于代表性域适应与PU学习方法；在2025年洛杉矶野火数据上，少标注损毁建筑条件下仍有效；进一步实验显示PANDA与GFM结合具备跨事件适应潜力。

Conclusion: PANDA对快速、可靠、跨事件的建筑损毁制图具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Mapping the damage to buildings following natural and human-caused disasters is essential for disaster response and humanitarian assistance. However, differences in hazard types, geographic environments, building characteristics, and imaging conditions cause cross-event distribution shifts that limit the generalization of deep learning models to new disaster events, even for geospatial foundation models (GFMs) pretrained on extensive Earth observation data. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) can mitigate these shifts, but adaptation remains challenging when damage-proportion and damage-appearance shifts coexist and interact. We identify enhancing target-domain discriminability as key to addressing this challenge. To this end, inspired by positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, we introduce positive-unlabeled domain adaptation, a paradigm that incorporates PU weak supervision to enhance target-domain discriminability, requiring only a few labeled damaged buildings in the target domain. Building upon this straightforward intuition, we develop Positive-ANchored Domain Adaptation (PANDA), comprising two complementary and mutually reinforcing components: Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness (PAIR) for discriminative representation learning and Refine Gate and Verifier (RAV) for supervision refinement. On a global-scale multi-disaster benchmark, PANDA generally outperforms representative domain adaptation and PU learning methods. Results on the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires show its effectiveness in new disaster scenarios with few labeled damaged buildings. Further experiments demonstrate the potential of integrating PANDA with GFMs for cross-event adaptation. These findings highlight PANDA's practical potential for rapid, reliable, and cross-event building damage mapping.

</details>


### [73] [Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology via Topology-Informed Graph Attention and Gene-Semantics Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33792)
*Yuqi Cheng,Boshuai Zhang,Ziqi Zhang,Xiuwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIGEM：拓扑感知、基因条件化的H&E→空间转录组预测框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组（ST）成本高，需从H&E图像推断表达；现有方法存在单patch嵌入不足（空间上下文导致形态相似区域表达不同，邻域聚合过度平滑边界）、固定基因索引输出头无法支持未见基因的严格零样本预测、常用指标难以衡量空间模式保真度与边界分辨率三大局限。

Method: 提出TIGEM：以组织级拓扑与形态线索正则化图注意力，学习拓扑感知的spot表示以避免跨区室平滑；用基因语义调制替代基因索引输出头，实现未见基因零样本预测；并引入图谱相关性（GSC）指标，作为PCC/MSE的补充评估空间模式保真度与边界分辨率。

Result: 在三个大规模ST数据集上取得整体SOTA性能，边界预测分辨率显著提升，并展示了对训练中未见基因表达模式的预测能力。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑感知空间传播与基于PLM的基因条件化，TIGEM拓展了先前的零样本组织学→ST工作，兼顾空间边界分辨率与未见基因泛化。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) profiles gene expression with spatial context but remains costly, motivating models that infer expression from H&E images. Existing approaches face three key limitations: (i) a single patch embedding is often insufficient: morphologically similar regions can exhibit distinct expression due to spatial context, while naive neighborhood aggregation can over-smooth boundaries; (ii) conventional fixed, gene-indexed output heads treat genes independently and cannot directly support strict zero-shot prediction for unseen genes; and (iii) common metrics under-measure spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution. To address these challenges, we propose TIGEM, a topology-informed, gene-conditioned framework for histology-to-ST inference. TIGEM learns topology-aware spot representations by regularizing graph attention with tissue-level topology and morphology cues to avoid cross-compartment smoothing, and uses gene semantic modulation to replace gene-indexed heads, enabling zero-shot prediction for unseen genes. We also introduce Graph Spectral Correlation (GSC) to evaluate spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution complement to PCC/MSE. Across three large-scale ST datasets, TIGEM achieves overall state-of-the-art performance and significantly improved boundary prediction resolution, and further demonstrates the ability to predict expression patterns for genes unseen during training, extending prior zero-shot histology-to-ST efforts with topology-aware spatial propagation and PLM-based gene conditioning.

</details>


### [74] [SprintGS: Eliminating Redundancy for Fast 3D Gaussian Splatting Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33796)
*Ryosuke Watanabe,Tomoaki Konno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SprintGS 在 FastGS 基础上从光栅化与损失计算两方面优化，实现 2.15 倍加速且质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 可实时渲染真实感新视角，但逐场景优化训练仍较慢。FastGS 等加速方法通过减少高斯数量将训练压缩到数分钟，但对其剖析发现即便在紧凑模型下仍有两项开销：光栅化仍是最大开销且存在冗余计算；损失计算不随高斯数量缩减，其在训练时间中的占比随模型紧凑化而上升。

Method: 提出 SprintGS 训练框架，保持模型紧凑并降低两类开销。光栅化方面：①调度式 tile 剔除，用粗略覆盖范围将每个高斯限制在真正重要的 tile 上，并在训练结束前退火至精确范围；②收敛感知剪枝，移除已收敛、近乎透明的高斯；③shuffle 高效反向传播，减少 GPU 线程间数据交换。损失方面：融合损失在前向中直接计算损失及其图像梯度，无需损失图，从而消除大部分开销。

Result: 在质量相当的情况下，SprintGS 训练速度比 FastGS 快 2.15 倍；高质量设置下快 1.81 倍。同时也能加速其他快速训练方法，以及动态、稀疏视角和表面重建任务。

Conclusion: SprintGS 通过针对光栅化冗余与损失计算两项瓶颈的系统性优化，在保持紧凑模型与渲染质量的前提下显著缩短 3DGS 训练时间，并具备良好的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders photorealistic novel views in real time, but its per-scene training (optimization) remains slow. Recent fast training methods such as FastGS shorten training to a few minutes by reducing the number of Gaussians. Profiling FastGS, we find two costs that remain even in its compact model. First, rasterization is still the largest cost, and redundant work remains in it. Second, the loss computation does not shrink with the number of Gaussians, so its share of the training time grows as the model becomes compact. We present SprintGS, a training framework built on FastGS that keeps the model compact and reduces both costs. For rasterization, scheduled tile culling limits each Gaussian to the tiles where it matters, using a coarse footprint that is annealed to the exact one before training ends; convergence-aware pruning removes converged, nearly transparent Gaussians; and a shuffle-efficient backward pass reduces the data exchanged between GPU threads. For the loss, a fused loss computes the loss and its image gradient in the forward pass without a loss map, removing most of its cost. SprintGS trains 2.15x faster than FastGS at comparable quality, and 1.81x faster in its high-quality setting. It also accelerates other fast training methods and dynamic, sparse-view, and surface reconstruction.

</details>


### [75] [DriftWAM: Faster World-Action Models via Action-Aware Video Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33797)
*Minyang Li,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Ying-Cong Chen,Chang Xu,Daochang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftWAM 是一个面向 world-action models 的少步蒸馏框架，通过引入动作感知的视频漂移与动作引导的视频正则，在保持推理速度的同时提升少步生成下的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-action models 统一了未来视频预测与动作生成，但迭代去噪带来高昂推理开销。将其蒸馏为少步生成器是高效部署的可行路径，但在激进步数压缩下维持任务性能困难。现有方法依赖逐点轨迹一致性蒸馏，追求高视觉保真度，却在优化视频生成时未利用下游动作信息，也缺乏显式的分布级对齐。

Method: DriftWAM 将预测视频所诱导的动作纳入视频学习：一是动作感知的视频漂移（action-aware video drifting），通过联合视觉—动作相似度核进行视频分布匹配，在诱导动作引导下驱动样本演化；二是动作引导的视频正则（action-guided video regularization），利用联合核识别出的教师—学生兼容匹配，将动作级梯度直接回传至视频生成器。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 LIBERO 基准以及真实机器人操作任务上的评测表明，在相同推理速度的少步设置下，DriftWAM 一致优于先前的 consistency distillation 方法。

Conclusion: 将下游动作信息与分布级对齐引入 WAM 的少步蒸馏，可在不增加推理成本的前提下提升任务表现，为 world-action models 的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) unify future video prediction and action generation for robotic control, but their reliance on iterative denoising incurs substantial inference costs. Distilling these models into few-step generators offers a promising route to efficient deployment, yet maintaining task performance under aggressive step reduction remains challenging. Existing methods rely on point-wise trajectory consistency distillation, which pursues high visual fidelity while leaving downstream action information unexploited in optimizing video generation, and also lacks explicit distribution-level alignment. To address this, we propose DriftWAM, a few-step WAM distillation framework that incorporates the actions induced by predicted videos into video learning. Specifically, DriftWAM integrates action-aware video drifting, which performs video distribution matching through a joint visual--action similarity kernel to steer sample evolution under the guidance of these induced actions, with action-guided video regularization, which leverages the compatible teacher--student matches identified by the joint kernel to backpropagate action-level gradients directly into the video generator. Evaluations on the RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO benchmarks, as well as in real-world robotic manipulation tasks, show that DriftWAM consistently outperforms prior consistency distillation methods across few-step settings at the same inference speed.

</details>


### [76] [Dynamic Forcing: Learning Action-Grounded Sparse Dynamic Representations with Dense Future Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33799)
*Ziqiang Li,Shisheng Chen,Yunnan Wang,Wenyao Zhang,Zili Lin,Yuyang Zhang,Xudong Yang,Fangzhuo Jin,Chenyu Zhou,Wenjun Zeng,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CompactWAM——稀疏-稠密联合预测的世界动作模型框架，用少量动态token表示未来变化，并配合Dynamic Forcing提升动作相关性，推理时仅需一次视觉前向，在LIBERO达98.4%成功率，仅用9.2%稠密token预算仍保持98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通过视觉预测支持机器人控制，但稠密未来建模会反复编码当前观测中已有的静态上下文，而真正与动作相关的变化在空间上往往稀疏，导致计算冗余。

Method: 研究稀疏动态表示学习：保留当前观测作为上下文锚点，用一小组紧凑动态token表示预期变化。构建CompactWAM，在视频监督的共享骨干内实现稀疏表示学习，且稀疏通路无需重建目标。提出Dynamic Forcing：以直接动作监督结合特权稠密未来引导，包括动作功能引导（匹配稀疏与稠密分支对动作专家的贡献）和动态感知分布匹配（对齐相对静态参考的时间关系变化）。推理时单次视觉前向即完成动作生成，无需稠密未来建模。

Result: LIBERO成功率98.4%，LIBERO-Plus 77.9%；在仅使用9.2%稠密未来token预算时，分别保持98.3%与76.6%。RoboTwin 2.0与真实世界操作实验进一步验证所学稀疏预测表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 以稀疏动态token替代稠密未来建模可在显著降低推理与表示开销的同时保持甚至接近稠密建模的性能，Dynamic Forcing是确保稀疏token动作相关性与动态保真度的关键。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) support robot control through visual prediction, but dense future modeling repeatedly encodes static context already available in the current observation, while action-relevant changes are often spatially sparse. We study sparse dynamic representation learning, retaining the current observation as a contextual anchor and using a small set of compact dynamic tokens to represent anticipated changes. We develop CompactWAM, a sparse-dense joint predictive framework that enables sparse representation learning within a video-supervised shared backbone, without requiring a reconstruction objective for the sparse pathway. Since a limited token budget alone does not specify which information to retain, we introduce Dynamic Forcing, which pairs direct action supervision with privileged dense future guidance to promote action relevance and dynamic fidelity. While action prediction gradients directly drive compact tokens to capture control-relevant cues, dense future guidance further shapes them via action functional guidance that matches sparse and dense contributions to the action expert, and dynamics-aware distribution matching, which aligns temporal relational changes relative to a static reference. At inference, a single visual forward pass encodes current observations and compact dynamic tokens for action generation, eliminating dense future modeling. CompactWAM achieves 98.4\% success on LIBERO and 77.9\% on LIBERO-Plus, retaining respective success rates of 98.3\% and 76.6\% with only 9.2\% of the dense future token budget. Evaluations on RoboTwin~2.0 and real-world manipulation further demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned sparse predictive representations.

</details>


### [77] [EvoVAU: Tool Evolution for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33808)
*Benfeng Wang,Chao Huang,Yudong Cheng,Chengliang Liu,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoVAU 提出一个为冻结视频求解器自动演化可复用工具库的视频异常理解框架：由外部提议器依据任务结果与执行轨迹迭代改进工具库，并采用跨版本对比反馈与采纳感知选择机制，在测试阶段固定演化后的工具库以完成多轮推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常理解需要推理事件演进、交互以及区分异常与常态的上下文条件，场景多样、推理需求复杂；现有视频智能体的预定义分析操作难以充分满足这些多样化推理需求。

Method: 将任务经验固化为可跨问题复用的分析操作：外部提议器基于任务结果与执行轨迹识别工具库局限并精炼；跨版本对比反馈结合当前错误与历史"纠正/回退"配对执行轨迹指导精炼；采纳感知选择仅在开发集整体准确率提升、且调用新/修改工具的问题中纠正数多于回退数时接受候选库；在小开发集上交替进行工具精炼与候选评估选择；测试阶段工具库固定，提议器不再参与。

Result: 在多个 VAU 基准上取得较强性能，并验证演化后工具库在跨异常领域的灵活性。

Conclusion: 通过演化可复用工具库，可在不更新冻结视频求解器的前提下提升多轮视频异常理解能力，且演化所得操作具备跨问题与跨异常领域的可迁移性。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) requires reasoning about event progression, interactions, and the contextual conditions that distinguish abnormal behavior from common activity across diverse scenes. Although video agents enable multi-round analysis, the predefined analytical operations may not adequately satisfy these diverse reasoning demands. In this paper, we present EvoVAU, a framework that evolves a reusable tool library for a frozen video solver. Guided by task outcomes and execution trajectories, an external proposer identifies potential limitations in the library and refines it, retaining task experience as analytical operations that can be reused across questions. Specifically, Cross-Version Contrastive Feedback guides tool refinement by combining current errors with paired execution trajectories from historical corrections and regressions. Adoption-Aware Selection accepts a candidate library only when overall development-set accuracy improves and corrections outnumber regressions among questions on which the solver invokes new or modified tools. Using a small development set, EvoVAU alternates tool refinement with candidate evaluation and selection. At test time, the evolved library remains fixed, with no further involvement from the proposer. Experiments on several VAU benchmarks demonstrate strong performance and the flexibility of the evolved library across anomaly domains.

</details>


### [78] [Probabilistic Monocular Biomechanical Reconstruction via Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33809)
*R. James Cotton,Fabian Sinz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生物力学流模型（BFM），用连续时间流匹配先验从单目视频重建3D关节运动学，在临床受试者上达到与确定性轨迹优化器相当的精度，并提供覆盖遮挡的可信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单视频重建3D关节运动学是病态问题：深度与身高歧义、肢体遮挡，且缺乏不确定性估计。

Method: BFM为连续时间流匹配先验，建模受试者人体测量学、解剖关节角轨迹与虚拟标记；在565名受试者的多相机临床记录上训练，测试时以单目2D关键点引导，无需配对视频训练。

Result: 在同一临床试验上与拟合相同骨架的SOTA确定性轨迹优化器对比，步行与TUG任务关节角精度相当；在留出的对照、假肢与神经科受试者上，步行与TUG平均绝对关节角误差为3.91°和4.97°，并能跨越时间性与整肢遮挡给出可信区间，关键点被遮挡时间隔随误差增大且覆盖率接近名义值。提供个体人体测量学校准可基本消除单目骨盆深度误差；在分布外的儿童蹲伏步态上预测有偏且过度自信。

Conclusion: BFM在保持精度同时提供校准良好的不确定性，个体人体测量学校准可解决骨盆深度歧义，但需病理多样化的运动先验以泛化到分布外步态。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D joint kinematics from a single video is ill-posed due to depth and stature ambiguity, limb occlusion, and missing uncertainty estimates. We introduce the Biomechanical Flow Model (BFM), a continuous-time flow-matching prior over subject anthropometry, anatomical joint-angle trajectories, and virtual markers, trained on multi-camera clinical recordings from 565 participants and steered toward monocular 2D keypoints at test time without paired video training. In a head-to-head comparison on the same clinical trials against a state-of-the-art deterministic trajectory optimizer fitting the identical skeleton, BFM achieves comparable joint-angle accuracy across walking and timed-up-and-go (TUG). Across held-out control, prosthetic, and neurologic participants, BFM reaches 3.91 deg and 4.97 deg mean absolute joint-angle error on walking and TUG while providing credible intervals that bridge temporal and whole-limb occlusions. Where keypoints are hidden, the intervals widen with the error and keep their coverage near nominal. Supplying individual anthropometric calibration removes most monocular pelvis depth error, whereas testing on out-of-distribution pediatric crouch gait produces biased, overconfident extension predictions, highlighting the need for pathologically diverse movement priors.

</details>


### [79] [FlipVLA: Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action(VLA) Models Only Through Bit Flips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33810)
*Zexi Zhao,Zizhen Liu,Jing Justin Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlipVLA，一种无需重训练、仅通过少量固定参数比特翻转即可在VLA模型中植入物体触发后门的两阶段"选择器-放大器"方法，在保持干净场景性能的同时实现90%以上攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型从视觉观测和语言指令生成机器人动作，恶意篡改会威胁物理安全。作者研究能否在不重训练的情况下，通过少量固定的参数比特翻转植入物体触发后门。核心难点在于：持久性的权重修改既要破坏触发存在时的行为，又要保持非触发输入上的性能。

Method: FlipVLA为两阶段选择器-放大器方法，利用FC-GELU-FC结构中已有的视觉特征。选择器在选定通道中构造触发相关响应，借助GELU衰减背景活动；放大器修改相连的下游权重，将响应转化为与动作相关的扰动。通过通道筛选与离散比特搜索，在有限的修改预算下构建两个组件。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT和GR00T N1.7上、跨四个LIBERO套件，攻击成功率达90.22%至100%，干净场景任务成功率下降最多1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，通过极少量比特翻转即可在VLA模型中高效植入物体触发后门，且几乎不影响正常性能，揭示了VLA模型的物理安全风险，凸显了参数级完整性防护的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. We study whether object-triggered backdoors can be implanted through a small, fixed set of parameter bit flips without retraining. The central challenge is to make persistent weight modifications disrupt trigger-present behavior while preserving performance on non-trigger inputs. We propose FlipVLA, a two-stage selector-amplifier method that exploits existing visual features within an FC-GELU-FC structure. The selector constructs a trigger-dependent response in a selected channel, using GELU to attenuate background activity. The amplifier modifies a connected downstream weight to convert this response into an action-relevant perturbation. Channel screening and discrete bit search construct both components under a limited modification budget. Experiments on OpenVLA-OFT and GR00T N1.7 across four LIBERO suites achieve attack success rates of 90.22% to 100%, with task success decreasing by at most 1.5 percentage points on clean scenes.

</details>


### [80] [MMotion: Collaborative Cross-Scale Memory for Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33819)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Qingchen Yu,Kejian Wu,Jian Yang,Zeming Liu,wenjun wu,Lili Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMotion——一个可插拔的协作式多尺度记忆框架，统一语义检索、潜在动力学建模与因果工作记忆，用于文本驱动的人体动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法要么依赖动作样例检索，要么仅保留长程上下文，很少利用记忆机制联合推理语义、动力学与生成历史，导致"生成什么动作、如何演化、如何保持历史一致性"三者难以协同。

Method: 设计三类互补记忆：语义记忆在不同生成尺度上提供动作先验；动力学记忆刻画潜在状态转移；工作记忆保存已生成动作的因果上下文。通过协作融合机制整合三类记忆以决定生成内容、演化方式与历史一致性。该框架与架构无关，可嵌入扩散模型与下一尺度自回归模型。

Result: 在标准基准与多种骨干网络上进行大量实验，相较强基线取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 多尺度记忆的协同利用可有效提升文本到动作生成的质量与一致性，且具备良好的架构通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation aims to synthesize realistic human motions from natural-language descriptions, enabling applications in animation, virtual avatars, and embodied agents. Despite recent progress, existing methods either retrieve motion exemplars or retain long-range context, but rarely exploit memory to jointly reason about semantics, dynamics, and generation history. In this paper, we propose MMotion, a collaborative multi-scale memory framework that unifies scale-conditioned semantic retrieval, latent dynamics modeling, and causal working memory. Specifically, semantic memory provides motion priors at different generation scales, dynamics memory captures latent state transitions, and working memory preserves causal context from previously generated motions. A collaborative fusion mechanism integrates these complementary memories to determine what motion to generate, how it should evolve, and how it should remain consistent with its history. MMotion is architecture-agnostic and can be readily integrated into both diffusion-based and next scale autoregressive models. Extensive experiments across standard benchmarks and diverse backbones demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Revisiting Monocular In-Hand Object Scanning: Dataset, Pose Estimation, and Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33824)
*Di Wu,Jeeeun Kim,Shu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ToysRUs数据集与基于子序列SfM拼接加遮挡感知2D高斯泼溅的单目视频手内物体扫描框架，性能超越神经隐式表示与3D基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手内物体扫描（IHOS）对非专业用户的3D数字化意义重大，但缺乏真实数据集与系统基准，相关研究不足；同时单目视频中相机位姿估计面临帧间小基线、大视角长程帧特征匹配稀疏以及动态手部遮挡等挑战。

Method: 构建ToysRUs数据集（4K UHD单目视频、多样小雕像）；从第一性原理分析SfM位姿估计问题；对按足够角度间隔采样的视频子序列分别做SfM，并迭代拼接子轨迹为统一全局轨迹；引入遮挡感知2D高斯泼溅，含不透明度渲染并在手部区域省略不透明度损失以提升表面重建保真度。

Result: 定量与定性评估表明，该框架在几何精度和光度保真度上均显著优于现有方法，包括神经隐式表示与3D基础模型。

Conclusion: 子序列采样SfM拼接与遮挡感知2DGS的组合能有效应对IHOS中的位姿估计与重建难题，ToysRUs为后续研究提供了数据与基准支撑。

Abstract: In-Hand Object Scanning (IHOS) from monocular video captured by a commodity smartphone holds immense potential to democratize 3D digitization for non-expert users, facilitating applications in digital archiving, augmented reality, and custom fabrication. Yet, IHOS remains largely understudied due to a lack of realistic datasets and systematic benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we introduce ToysRUs, a high-quality dataset featuring diverse figurines captured by 4K Ultra High-Definition (UHD) monocular videos. Studying IHOS from first principles, we examine structure-from-motion (SfM) for camera pose estimation, which is key to IHOS. We identify two key challenges: small baselines between consecutive frames, and sparse feature matches across large-view, long-range frames, further exacerbated by dynamic hand occlusions. Guided by these insights, we propose performing SfM on video sub-sequences sampled at sufficient angular intervals, and iteratively stitches the corresponding sub-trajectories into a unified global trajectory. Furthermore, for high-fidelity surface reconstruction, we introduce occlusion-aware 2D Gaussian Splatting, which incorporates opacity rendering and omits the opacity loss on hand regions. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing approaches, including neural implicit representations and 3D foundation models, in both geometric accuracy and photometric fidelity.

</details>


### [82] [MotionODE: Enhancing Autoregressive Text-to-Motion Generation with Continuous-Depth Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33826)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Jian Yang,Kejian Wu,Zeming Liu,Wenjun Wu,Lili Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MotionODE：将预训练自回归文本驱动动作生成器中选定的Transformer残差分支视为局部向量场，用中点Runge–Kutta(RK2)积分替换原始更新，并通过混合一步/两步RK2后训练实现连续深度式隐藏状态演化，在HumanML3D上提升文本–动作对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归动作生成需要局部预测同时兼顾跨时段文本约束与已有动作上下文，但现有工作多聚焦于动作表示与生成顺序，条件动作表示的内部形成过程研究不足。

Method: MotionODE是一种连续深度后训练框架：把选定Transformer残差分支重新解释为局部向量场，以RK2中点积分替代原更新，保持动作表示与外层生成机制不变；针对预训练更新路径与新引入中间状态的不匹配，采用动作token监督下的混合一步/两步RK2后训练，并可选蒸馏与更新映射约束以保留预训练行为；无需从头训练生成器，兼容next-token与next-scale生成。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，于MoScale、BAMM、LMR三个基线上均取得一致的文本–动作对齐改进。

Conclusion: 内部表示更新是改进自回归动作生成的一个互补方向，且该框架具备最小侵入性与跨生成范式通用性。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-motion generation requires local motion predictions to reconcile cross-temporal textual requirements with the available motion context. While existing methods primarily improve motion representations and generation order, the internal formation of conditioned motion representations remains less explored. We introduce MotionODE, a continuous-depth post-training framework that refines hidden-state evolution in pretrained autoregressive generators. MotionODE reinterprets selected Transformer residual branches as local vector fields and replaces their original updates with midpoint Runge--Kutta (RK2) integration, while preserving the motion representation and outer generation mechanism. To address the mismatch between pretrained update paths and newly introduced intermediate states, we propose mixed one-step/two-step RK2 post-training under motion-token supervision, with optional distillation and update-map constraints to preserve pretrained behavior. This minimally invasive framework supports both next-token and next-scale generation without training the generator from scratch. Extensive experiments on HumanML3D demonstrate consistent improvements in text--motion alignment across MoScale, BAMM and LMR, highlighting internal representation updates as a complementary direction for improving autoregressive motion generation.

</details>


### [83] [3D CoCa v3: Contrastive Learners with Scene Graphs for Urban Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33835)
*Nannan Zhang,Ting Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Yanjie Zhu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 3D CoCa v3，一个场景图增强的对比-生成统一框架，用于大规模城市 3D 场景理解，并构建 City3D-9.6K 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 视觉-语言方法偏重全局语义对齐或物体级表示，缺乏统一对比与生成框架下的结构化关系建模，难以刻画多尺度几何结构与复杂空间关系。

Method: Pyramid Vision Encoder 进行多尺度几何表示学习；基于语言模型的解码器，含问题引导的 caption 检索与视觉交叉注意力；引入基于 MSG 的关系描述以将结构化空间关系融入对比式 3D-语言对齐；构建 City3D-9.6K 多视角城市视觉-语言数据集（视觉-几何观测、场景 caption、场景图对齐）。

Result: 在 City-3DQA 上，sentence-wise 与 city-wise 划分的总体 top-1 准确率分别为 88.35% 和 64.79%，较 3D CoCa v2 提升 20.88 与 3.57 个百分点；在 GeoEval3D 上取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 场景图增强的关系监督与多尺度几何表示可有效提升城市 3D 空间推理与几何理解能力，验证了对比-生成统一框架的优越性。

Abstract: Understanding large-scale 3D urban scenes requires representations that capture both multi-scale geometric structures and complex spatial relationships. However, existing 3D vision-language methods primarily emphasize global semantic alignment or object-level representations, leaving structured relational modeling underexplored within unified contrastive and generative frameworks. In this paper, we present 3D CoCa v3, a scene-graph-enhanced contrastive and generative framework for urban 3D scene understanding. Our framework features a Pyramid Vision Encoder for multi-scale geometric representation learning and a language-model-based decoder with question-guided caption retrieval and visual cross-attention. We further introduce MSG-based relational descriptions to enrich contrastive 3D-language alignment with structured spatial relationships. To support semantic and relational supervision, we construct City3D-9.6K, a multi-view urban vision-language dataset with aligned visual-geometric observations, scene captions, and scene graphs. Extensive experiments on City-3DQA demonstrate that 3D CoCa v3 achieves 88.35% and 64.79% overall top-1 accuracy on the sentence-wise and city-wise splits, surpassing 3D CoCa v2 by 20.88 and 3.57 percentage points, respectively. Furthermore, 3D CoCa v3 achieves the best performance on GeoEval3D, demonstrating its effectiveness in urban spatial reasoning and geometric understanding. Code is available at https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/3DCoCav3.

</details>


### [84] [The Missing Root: Geometric Imagination Without Images in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33841)
*Loïc Magnan,Tomasz Kozlowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将连续物体令牌（位姿+符号距离形状嵌入）与词令牌置于同一Transformer序列，配合控制令牌与注意力掩码，使语言模型直接从持久几何工作区读取空间信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型仅在离散词元上运算，只能"用语言猜几何"；多数多模态方法把空间 grounding 等同于图像/视频帧，但空间结构本身不是图像，而是坐标系、实体边界及物体在连续空间中的运动与关系。因此需要新范式让模型直接操作连续空间表示。

Method: 在单一不间断的transformer序列中，将连续物体令牌（联合编码位置、朝向、尺度的位姿与有符号距离场形状嵌入）与词令牌并列，实现语言与几何的相互注意力；控制令牌在自然语言输出与对持久物体工作区的连续状态更新之间自动切换；注意力掩码在生成答案时屏蔽先前文本，迫使回答直接读取已确定的几何状态。实现上在未冻结的Qwen3-1.7B层旁挂接一个窄并行空间流（仅增4.4%参数）。

Result: 在二维测试中，模型能依据英文描述生成并变换多物体场景，将形状迁移到未见名词，且因果性地依赖工作区来回答空间问题。

Conclusion: 以连续物体令牌+持久几何工作区替代"用语言描述几何"，可在低参数开销下为LLM赋予可验证、因果有效的空间理解能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new paradigm and method for giving large language models spatial understanding. Standard language models operate exclusively over discrete words (secondary representations of things rather than continuous space itself), forcing them to guess geometry verbally. Whereas most multimodal approaches equate spatial grounding with camera images or video frames, spatial structure itself is not a picture: it consists of coordinate frames, solid boundaries, and how objects move and relate to each other in continuous space. Motivated by this distinction, we place continuous object tokens combining pose (position, orientation, and scale) and signed-distance shape embeddings alongside word tokens inside a single uninterrupted transformer sequence, allowing language and geometry to attend to each other as object scenes are built and updated step by step. Control tokens switch output automatically between natural-language words and continuous object state updates to a persistent object workspace, while an attention mask blocks earlier prose when generating the answer, so responses must be read directly from settled geometry. To prove this method works, we attach a narrow parallel spatial stream (+4.4% weights) alongside unfrozen Qwen3-1.7B layers. In our two-dimensional tests, the model spawns and transforms multi-object scenes from English descriptions, transfers shapes to held-out nouns, and relies causally on the workspace to answer spatial questions.

</details>


### [85] [SPiXSeg: Rethinking MLLM-based Image Segmentation as Superpixel Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33842)
*Piyush Tiwary,Sohini Dutta,Prathosh A. P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPiXSeg 将 MLLM 的分割输出从 patch 级标签分配改为 superpixel 级标签分配：用现成模型一次性生成任务无关的超像素划分，再通过 S2M 模块在单次前向中比较隐藏表示给每个超像素 token 打分，无需解码器头或后处理精修即可获得高精度语言引导分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MLLM 的分割方法存在两难：使用解码器需额外可训练头；patch 级方法在边界精度与预测数量之间权衡——粗网格混合前景/背景像素导致边界粗糙，细网格预测数量激增；自回归掩码生成还带来串行解码开销。作者希望找到一种兼顾边界精度与效率的输出范式。

Method: 提出 SPiXSeg：(1) 用现成模型将图像划分为任务无关的超像素（partition 只计算一次，跨任务复用）；(2) 聚合 patch 级视觉特征形成超像素 token；(3) Spix-to-Mask (S2M) 模块在单次 MLLM 前向中直接比较 MLLM 隐藏表示对每个超像素 token 打分，无需自回归生成或独立打分网络；(4) 选中的超像素直接映射回像素，无任何精修。整个流程不含可训练解码器头与后处理。

Result: 在 RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg 上平均 cIoU 达 80.5（LLaVA-1.5-7B）与 81.9（InternVL2.5-8B）；仅在 COCO-Stuff 上训练即可扩展到开放词表语义分割，在 ADE20K、PASCAL Context、PASCAL VOC 上 mIoU 分别为 29.0、59.5、95.4，优于多个 SoTA MLLM 基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一个固定的、与查询无关的超像素划分已足以支撑准确的语言引导分割，说明分割输出的粒度选择（超像素而非 patch）本身即为提升 MLLM 分割精度与效率的关键设计，且无需解码器头或后处理精修。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for image segmentation. However, existing methods either use a decoder or use patch-based approach which trades boundary precision against the number of decisions as coarse grid cells mix foreground and background pixels, while finer grids require many more predictions, and autoregressive mask generation adds sequential decoding overhead. We propose SPiXSeg, which shifts the output from per-patch to per-superpixel label assignment, using superpixel tokens formed by aggregating patch-level visual features. An off-the-shelf model partitions each image into task-agnostic superpixels, so the partition is computed once and reused across tasks. A Spix-to-Mask (S2M) module scores each superpixel token in a single MLLM forward pass by directly comparing the MLLM's hidden representations, without autoregressive generation or a separate scoring network, and the selected superpixels are mapped directly to pixels without refinement. Without any trainable decoder head or post-hoc refinement, SPiXSeg achieves an average cIoU of 80.5 with LLaVA-1.5-7B and 81.9 with InternVL2.5-8B on RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg and, when trained only on COCO-Stuff, extends to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation with mIoU of 29.0, 59.5 and 95.4 on ADE20K, PASCAL Context and PASCAL VOC, outperforming several SoTA MLLM baselines. Our results suggest that a fixed, query-agnostic superpixel partition is enough for accurate language-guided segmentation.

</details>


### [86] [Continuous Foundation-Guided Pseudo-Label Weighting for Breast Ultrasound Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33843)
*Xinyu Fan,Xinyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究连续式基础模型引导的伪标签加权用于乳腺超声分割的无监督域适应（UDA）：冻结的AutoSAMUS先验以[0.5,1]有界权重调节EMA教师伪标签的监督强度，在五个公开数据集、十一个源-目标方向上评估，整体优于全像素EMA与原BiRP，但BrEaST源结果混合，且属探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺超声分割模型在扫描仪、机构与人群间泛化差；既有选择性伪标签规则（BiRP）仅监督部分目标像素，且需测试时调用基础模型，故需一种对全部目标像素连续加权、且无需测试期基础模型推理的UDA方案。

Method: EMA教师在目标域生成伪标签；冻结的超声分割基础模型AutoSAMUS提供先验，用有界权重（先验确定性与先验-教师概率一致性之组合）调制每个目标像素的监督贡献，权重区间[0.5,1]，无硬性像素筛选；在TransUNet与U-Net两套流程上，跨三个源域、十一个源-目标方向评测。

Result: TransUNet协议下，连续加权在BUSI源的全部四个目标上优于全像素EMA与原BiRP；BUS-BRA源的全部四个目标上取得最高平均Dice与最低HD95；BrEaST源结果混合（BUSI最优，OASBUD与原BiRP相当，BUS-UCLM上所有域适应方法均不及源域单独训练）。U-Net流程中仅用一致性加权与完整公式性能相当，先验确定性的增量价值未确定。

Conclusion: 连续有界加权是有效的EMA伪标签调制策略，多数方向优于全像素EMA与选择性BiRP且免除测试期基础模型推理；但增益依源域而变、先验确定性作用未证实，且加权规则在部分目标测试结果揭盲后制定，故定位为探索性研究并如实报告不利结果。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound segmentation models often generalize poorly across scanners, institutions, and patient populations. We investigate continuous foundation-guided pseudo-label weighting for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). An exponential-moving-average (EMA) teacher generates target-domain pseudo-labels, while a frozen ultrasound segmentation model (AutoSAMUS) modulates their contribution using a bounded weight that combines the prior's certainty with its probabilistic agreement with the teacher. In contrast to our earlier selective BiRP rule, the proposed formulation supervises every target pixel with a weight in [0.5, 1] and requires no foundation-model inference at test time. We evaluate the method on five public breast ultrasound datasets, spanning three source domains and eleven source-target directions. Under the TransUNet protocol, continuous weighting outperforms both all-pixel EMA and original BiRP on all four BUSI-source targets, and achieves the highest mean Dice and lowest HD95 among the compared methods on all four BUS-BRA-source targets. Results with BrEaST as the source are mixed: continuous weighting performs best on BUSI and comparably to original BiRP on OASBUD, whereas none of the adaptation methods surpasses source-only training on BUS-UCLM. In a separate U-Net pipeline, agreement-only weighting performs comparably to the full formulation, leaving the incremental value of prior certainty unresolved. Because the weighting rule was developed after some target-test results had been examined, we treat the study as exploratory and report adverse as well as favorable outcomes.

</details>


### [87] [Hybrid Reinforcement Learning for One-step Degraded Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33857)
*Hongwei Yu,Daoqing Zha,Jiawei Li,Yancheng Bai,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一步式融合框架OneHRL，将退化输入视为生成轨迹的中间噪声态，用蒸馏式一步生成器直接矫正为清晰融合结果，并结合Flow-GRPO注入随机性以缓解过平滑，配以由粗到细的混合奖励提升融合质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外与可见光图像融合（IVIF）对鲁棒感知至关重要，但现有方法在复杂退化下表现受限。扩散模型虽能应对退化，却因迭代采样带来高计算成本；一步蒸馏虽可加速推理，却常导致结果过平滑、丢失细节纹理。

Method: 1）将退化输入视为生成轨迹中的中间噪声状态，训练蒸馏式一步生成器直接将其矫正为干净融合结果；2）引入Flow-GRPO范式，为确定性一步生成注入随机性，恢复基于采样的强化学习所需的随机探索；3）设计由粗到细的奖励，融合全局语义奖励与目标级（object-level）奖励，构成混合奖励。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在融合质量与计算效率上均优于现有方法，兼具自然性与细粒度纹理保持能力。

Conclusion: OneHRL通过"一步蒸馏+强化学习随机化+混合奖励"的组合，在保持一步推理高效性的同时克服蒸馏过平滑问题，实现了高质量、高效率的红外与可见光图像融合。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) is essential for robust perception, yet existing methods remain limited under complex degradations. While diffusion models (DMs) offer powerful modeling capabilities to combat such degradations, their iterative process incurs high computational cost. One-step distillation effectively accelerates inference but typically leads to over-smoothed results. To resolve these limitations, we propose a one-step fusion framework with hybrid reinforcement learning, termed OneHRL. We treat degraded inputs as intermediate noisy states along a generative trajectory, enabling a distilled one-step generator to rectify them directly to the clean fusion results. Concurrently, to mitigate the over-smoothed results in distillation, we incorporate the Flow-GRPO paradigm to introduce stochasticity into the deterministic one-step generation. This restores the stochastic exploration necessary for sampling-based reinforcement learning. Furthermore, we design a coarse-to-fine reward that integrates a global semantic reward with an object-level reward. This hybrid reward ensures that the generator captures both naturalness and fine-grained texture. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior fusion quality and efficiency.

</details>


### [88] [Structured Noise Diffusion for Echocardiography Haze Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33860)
*Zike Luo,Hao Li,Mengxiao Wang,Qingya Li,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知扩散框架，将超声雾霾建模为受扇形几何约束的局部高斯分量叠加，实现回声图像去雾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图常受空间结构化雾霾伪影退化，遮挡解剖边界；常规扩散模型使用像素独立高斯噪声，难以刻画局部化的超声雾霾。

Method: 前向过程通过采样高斯分量的位置与尺度生成空间相关退化，并以超声扇形几何约束；反向恢复采用双头网络，分别预测锚点概率图与高斯尺度参数，逐步去除结构化伪影，同时缓解直接坐标回归带来的中心偏置。

Result: 在公开DehazingEcho2025基准及两个私有临床数据集上评估，均未对私有队列重训练或微调；三个数据集上平均广义对比噪声比（gCNR）均为最高，公开基准达0.715±0.090，并保持组织边缘。

Conclusion: 以结构化高斯雾霾建模替代像素独立噪声的扩散框架能更贴合真实超声伪影，去雾性能与跨域泛化性优于对比方法。

Abstract: Echocardiographic images are frequently degraded by spatially structured haze artifacts that obscure anatomical boundaries and hinder clinical analysis. Conventional diffusion models typically employ pixel-independent Gaussian noise, limiting their ability to represent localized ultrasound haze. In this study, we propose a structure-informed diffusion framework that explicitly models ultrasound haze as an accumulation of localized Gaussian components constrained by the ultrasound fan geometry. The forward process generates spatially correlated corruption by sampling component locations and scales, providing a formulation that better reflects the spatial characteristics of real echo artifacts. During reverse restoration, a dual-head network predicts an anchor probability map and Gaussian scale parameters to progressively remove structured artifacts while reducing the center bias associated with direct coordinate regression. We evaluated the framework on the public DehazingEcho2025 benchmark and two private clinical datasets, without retraining or fine-tuning on either private cohort. Our method achieved the highest mean generalized contrast-to-noise ratio across all three datasets, reaching $0.715 \pm 0.090$ on the public benchmark while preserving tissue edges.

</details>


### [89] [OmniMeld: Enhancing Audio-Visual Understanding via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33861)
*Daili Hua,Yifan Dai,Bohan Zeng,Yuran Wang,Zihao Wu,Chengzhuo Tong,Guoqing Jiang,Liwei Chen,Zhiheng Qin,Xuanping Li,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniMeld：面向音视频理解的多教师on-policy蒸馏方法，用模态-响应引导的最优传输分布匹配替代直接概率匹配，并构建OmniMeld-20K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频理解需感知对齐、时间理解与语义推理等多重能力，多教师蒸馏可整合这些专长，但直接概率匹配无法显式刻画教师预测在音视频条件变化下的响应差异。

Method: 训练四个覆盖不同能力域的教师（grounding与对应关系、时序动态与事件结构、因果推理、上下文整体理解）；依据学生生成的答案前缀将样本路由至对应教师；以教师预测在共享候选token上随音视频条件变化的改变量构造传输代价，用最优传输使学生预测对齐完整音视频输入下的教师分布；并构建OmniMeld-20K，响应结构化为evidence/reasoning/answer并经一致性检验与媒体审查。

Result: 教师在同规模开源模型中具竞争力；学生在全部评测基准上优于基座模型，并在若干基准上超过教师的基座模型。

Conclusion: 模态-响应引导的传输式分布匹配可有效整合多域教师能力，实现音视频理解的学生模型稳定增益。

Abstract: Audio-visual understanding requires perceptual grounding, temporal understanding, and semantic inference. Multi-teacher distillation can consolidate these capabilities, but direct probability matching does not explicitly capture teacher prediction changes across audio-visual conditions. We introduce OmniMeld, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation method that integrates domain expertise through modality-response-guided distribution matching. We train four teachers covering grounding and correspondence, temporal dynamics and event structure, causal and inferential reasoning, and contextual and holistic understanding. Each sample is routed to its corresponding teacher for supervision conditioned on student-generated answer prefixes. We derive transport costs from teacher prediction changes over shared candidate tokens across audio-visual conditions, using optimal transport to match student predictions to those of the teacher under complete audio-visual input. We also construct OmniMeld-20K for domain-specialized teacher training. The dataset spans four capability domains, with responses structured into evidence, reasoning, and answer sections and verified through consistency checks and media-based review. Experiments on audio-visual and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate competitive teacher performance among similarly sized open-source models. The student improves over its base model across all evaluated benchmarks and surpasses the teachers' base model on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [MAFNet-UR$^3$: Mask-Aware Fusion Network for Unified Raindrop and Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33866)
*Yeongjin Jeong,Hyunjin Cho,Minsang Kim,Jiwon Lee,Jae Woong Soh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架MAFNet-UR³，利用多观测实现场景一致的雨滴与反射统一去除（UR³），在RDRF-testing上超越DiffUR³（PSNR +2.33 dB），并在LoViF 2026 UR³挑战赛获第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挡风玻璃上雨滴与反射常同时出现并共同遮挡场景内容，统一去除（UR³）高度病态。现有基于扩散模型的方法虽能生成视觉上合理的复原结果，但在严重受损区域易产生与场景不一致的结构。

Method: MAFNet-UR³采用三阶段框架：Stage I 抑制雨滴同时保留反射层；Stage II 先做先验引导的反射去除，再进行图像空间细化；Stage III 从中间复原结果估计退化掩膜以提供退化线索，并对多观测进行掩膜感知的多尺度融合，以保持场景结构与细节。

Result: 在RDRF-testing基准上取得SOTA，相比DiffUR³ PSNR提升2.33 dB、challenge score提升2.7634；在LoViF 2026 UR³ Challenge中排名第三，最终得分41.1416。

Conclusion: 通过多观测融合与显式退化掩膜引导，可有效缓解扩散类方法的结构不一致问题，实现场景一致的雨滴与反射统一去除，验证了多阶段、多观测策略的有效性。

Abstract: Raindrops and reflections frequently co-occur on windshields and jointly obscure scene content, making their unified removal, referred to as UR$^3$, a highly ill-posed problem. Existing approaches are mainly based on diffusion models and thus can produce visually plausible restorations. However, they may generate scene-inconsistent structures in severely corrupted regions. To address this limitation, we propose MAFNet-UR$^3$, which adopts a three-stage framework to exploit multiple observations for scene-consistent unified raindrop and reflection removal. Specifically, Stage I suppresses raindrops while preserving the reflection layer, and Stage II performs prior-guided reflection removal followed by image-space refinement. Stage III estimates degradation masks from intermediate restoration results to provide degradation cues and performs mask-aware multi-scale fusion across observations to preserve scene structure and fine details. As a result, our proposed MAFNet-UR$^3$-L achieves state-of-the-art performance on the RDRF-testing benchmark, outperforming DiffUR$^3$ by 2.33 dB in PSNR and 2.7634 in the challenge score. Our method ranked third in the LoViF 2026 UR$^3$ Challenge, achieving a final score of 41.1416.

</details>


### [91] [Concept Generalization in Visual Brain Decoding via Encoder-Generated fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33870)
*Peter Yongho Kim,Juhyeon Park,Dongsheng Li,Yansen Wang,Dongqi Han,Mingqing Xiao,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉脑解码器在语义分布相似的数据上训练评测，泛化到未见概念的能力不明；作者研究概念泛化问题，发现编码器比解码器更鲁棒，并用编码器生成合成fMRI数据补充训练，显著恢复解码器在未见概念上的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI-to-image解码器通常在相似语义分布上训练和评估，对未见过的语义概念的泛化能力缺乏认识；且获取新概念fMRI数据成本高、耗时长。作者希望通过"概念留出"实验刻画泛化缺陷，并寻找无需额外采集数据的补救方案。

Method: 在目标被试训练数据中留出若干语义概念（跨3个解码器、4名被试、12个概念）。对比fMRI-to-image解码器与直接将预训练视觉表征映射到体素的脑编码器在相同留出下的鲁棒性差异；进而用编码器为新图像生成合成fMRI响应，构成"图像-fMRI"对加入解码器训练。并通过编码器训练条件、编码器设计及合成数据对照实验，检验增益来源。

Result: 三种解码器在留出概念上性能显著下降，即使该概念在多被试模型中仍可用；而脑编码器在相同语义留出下明显更鲁棒。将编码器生成的合成数据加入训练后，可在三个解码器、四名被试、十二个概念上恢复相当比例的损失性能，且无需额外fMRI采集；在广泛数据预算下也提升整体解码性能。对照实验表明增益依赖编码器的泛化能力与合成监督中的语义内容，而非单纯增加数据量。

Conclusion: 编码器比解码器具有更好的语义泛化性，其生成的合成fMRI可把语义泛化能力迁移给解码器，为低成本提升脑解码器对未见概念的泛化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Visual brain decoders are typically trained and evaluated on stimuli drawn from similar semantic distributions, leaving their ability to reconstruct unseen concepts poorly understood. We study this concept generalization problem by withholding semantic concepts from a target subject's training data. We find that three recent fMRI-to-image decoders degrade substantially for the held-out concepts, even when the concept remains available for multi-subject models. In contrast, brain encoders that map a pretrained visual representation directly to voxels are markedly more robust to the same semantic holdout. Motivated by this asymmetry, we use such an encoder to generate synthetic fMRI responses for new images and add the resulting image-fMRI pairs to decoder training. Across three decoders, four subjects, and twelve semantic concepts, synthetic data recovers a substantial fraction of the performance lost on unseen concepts without requiring additional, time-consuming fMRI acquisition. We further show that the same approach improves general decoding performance across a wide range of data budgets. Analyses of encoder training conditions, encoder designs, and synthetic-data controls show that the gains depend on both encoder generalization and the semantic content of the synthetic supervision, rather than merely increasing the amount of training data. Together, our results show that encoder-generated fMRI can transfer semantic generalization to decoders.

</details>


### [92] [X-Matte: VAE-Free Pixel-Space Flow Matching for Single-Step Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33874)
*Zanyi Wang,Jiaang Li,Dengyang Jiang,Ziru Wang,Chengzu Li,Davit Abrahamyan,Harry Yang,Serge Belongie,Mengmeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Matte：一种免VAE、像素空间流匹配的单步图像抠图框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练生成模型用于抠图时依赖VAE，其空间压缩会损害精细alpha细节并带来额外推理开销。

Method: 直接在像素空间将RGB到alpha的转换建模为连续流，采用轨迹级x-prediction与过渡感知的alpha监督，单次前向预测目标alpha。

Result: 在P3M基准上与最先进的无trimap抠图方法相当，并向RWP-636和AIM-500展现良好零样本泛化；高分辨率下相比单步隐空间对照模型最高9.4倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 免VAE的像素空间建模可实现准确且高效的图像抠图。

Abstract: Pre-trained generative models offer rich visual priors for image matting, but their adaptation often relies on variational autoencoders (VAEs), whose spatial compression can degrade fine alpha details and introduce additional inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose X-Matte, a VAE-free pixel-space flow matching framework for single-step image matting. By eliminating latent compression, X-Matte formulates the RGB-to-alpha transition as a continuous flow directly in pixel space. We adopt trajectory-wide x-prediction with transition-aware alpha supervision, enabling the model to learn from intermediate states while directly predicting the target alpha matte in a single forward pass. This design avoids both VAE encoding and decoding and iterative sampling. Extensive experiments demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art trimap-free matting methods on P3M benchmarks, together with promising zero-shot generalization to RWP-636 and AIM-500. Moreover, X-Matte achieves up to a 9.4x inference speedup over a constructed single-step latent counterpart at high resolution, demonstrating the effectiveness of VAE-free pixel-space modeling for accurate and efficient image matting.

</details>


### [93] [Off the Driven Path: Transferable Road Understanding in Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33878)
*Lena Wild,Christian Helten,Katie Z Luo,Jakob Thumm,Rafael Valencia,Patric Jensfelt,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Road-KSU 框架，将 HD 地图与目标标注自动转为路中心场景图，生成道路聚焦问答/推理目标与地图轨迹合规指标；结合地理不相交划分，交叉"输出模态×地点熟悉度"评估，揭示端到端驾驶规划器依赖记忆化道路几何，驾驶分数提升不等同于可迁移的道路理解，地理多样性是迁移的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略在推理时日益不依赖显式 HD 地图，但其内部究竟内化了多少道路知识尚不清楚；现有评估以动作空间指标为主，难以直接探查模型对道路结构的知识，且数据划分常存在地理重叠，可能高估泛化能力。

Method: 构建 Road-KSU：自动将 HD 地图与 actor 标注转换为路中心场景图，派生出道路聚焦的视觉问答与推理任务，并配合基于地图的轨迹合规性指标。采用地理不相交（geographically disjoint）数据划分，形成 2×2 评估设计：输出模态（语言 vs. 动作）×地点熟悉度（已见 vs. 未见道路）。在 NAVSIM 上分析 13 个已发表规划器在转弯处的得分随训练经过次数的变化，并通过重训练与数据规模/覆盖实验进行因果验证。

Result: NAVSIM 测试样本中 95% 距某训练样本 10m 以内；13 个规划器在转弯处的得分随训练经过该地点的次数急剧上升，暗示道路几何记忆。地理不相交重训练后，动作空间性能在未见转弯处下降最大，失败以几何合规性为主；语言侧，道路中心 VQA 在直道与弯道上均下降，而在动态场景内容上保持稳定。进一步扩数据呈双重分离：在相同地点增加行驶次数可改善未见道路上的驾驶，但道路问答不变；固定预算下分散到更多地点则可提升道路问答，而驾驶性能变化很小。

Conclusion: 驾驶分数的提升并不必然意味着在未见道路上具备更广的道路理解；动作与语言两种输出模态对地理覆盖与数据量的敏感性呈双向分离。地理多样性是获得可迁移道路知识的关键，Road-KSU 为诊断端到端驾驶策略的道路知识提供了可复用的评估工具。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies increasingly operate without explicit HD maps at inference, raising the question of what road knowledge they actually internalize. To probe this knowledge through model outputs, we introduce Road-KSU, a framework that automatically converts HD maps and actor annotations into a road-centric scene graph. From this, we derive road-focused question answering and reasoning targets, complemented by map-based trajectory compliance metrics. Pairing Road-KSU with geographically disjoint splits yields a $2 \times 2$ evaluation crossing output modality (language vs. action) with place familiarity (seen vs. unseen roads). On NAVSIM, 95% of test samples lie within 10m of a training sample, and scores of 13 published planners on turns rise steeply with the number of training drives through the test location, pointing to memorized road geometry. Retraining on geographically disjoint splits and probing with Road-KSU confirm that action-space performance degrades most on unfamiliar turns, with failures dominated by geometric compliance. Language accuracy, however, drops on road-centric VQA on straight and curved roads alike, and remains stable on dynamic scene content. Scaling the training data further reveals a double dissociation: more drives through the same places improve driving on unseen roads but leave road question answering unchanged, whereas spreading a fixed data budget over more places increases road question answering with little change in driving performance. These findings show that improved driving scores need not imply broader road understanding on unseen roads, and identify geographic diversity as key to transferable road knowledge.

</details>


### [94] [LayoutRecall: Preserving Appearance in Multi-Round 3D Layout-Controlled Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33885)
*Yankuan Chi,Xin Lin,Baihan Yang,Yuheng Liu,Jason Li,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LayoutRecall，一种在3D布局控制的多轮图像编辑中，将物体外观存储于3D框局部坐标系并跨轮复用，以解决遮挡或出视野后外观丢失的问题；同时提出多轮编辑基准 LayoutLoop。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D布局控制的图像编辑可显式指定物体位置与相机视角，但在连续编辑序列中，物体被遮挡或离开视野后，最新图像会丢失其外观信息。目标布局只规定物体应重新出现的位置，而外观必须从早期观测中恢复，现有方法难以在几何一致的方式下完成这种跨轮外观回溯。

Method: 1) Box-Surface Latent Memory：将图像潜变量存于3D框表面，并沿目标视角射线检索；2) Geometry-Aligned Memory Conditioning：以目标视角位置、共享的框局部嵌入和物体级注意力，将检索到的token注入FLUX.2编辑器；3) 对应性损失(correspondence loss)，引导去噪过程中关注匹配的历史观测；4) 构建 LayoutLoop 基准，含校准布局与刻意设计的重访(revisit)设置。

Result: 在 LayoutLoop 及真实场景数据上进行大量实验，验证了方法恢复被遮挡或出视野物体外观、并保持3D布局一致性的能力。

Conclusion: 通过在3D框局部坐标系中显式记忆并几何对齐地复用外观，LayoutRecall 有效解决了多轮3D布局编辑中的外观遗忘问题；LayoutLoop 为该问题提供了带校准布局与重访场景的评测基准。

Abstract: 3D layout-controlled image editing gives users explicit control over object placement and camera viewpoint. In a sequence of edits, however, the latest image can lose the appearance of an object when it is occluded or leaves view. The target layout specifies where the object should reappear, while its appearance must be recovered from earlier observations. We introduce LayoutRecall, a multi-round editor that stores and reuses appearance in the local coordinate systems of 3D boxes. Box-Surface Latent Memory retains image latents on box faces and retrieves them along rays in the target view. Geometry-Aligned Memory Conditioning supplies the retrieved tokens to a FLUX.2 editor using target-view positions, shared box-local embeddings, and object-wise attention. A correspondence loss encourages the editor to attend to matching observations during denoising. We also introduce LayoutLoop, a multi-round 3D layout-controlled image editing benchmark with calibrated layouts and designed revisits. We conduct extensive experiments on LayoutLoop and real-scene data to verify the ability of our method. The project page is at https://ykchizs.github.io/layoutrecall.github.io/.

</details>


### [95] [DeltaRAE: Derivative Tokenization for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33886)
*Mingzhuo Yu,Shengcao Cao,Chengzhi Mao,Yong Cheng,Di Jiang,Lijun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaRAE 是一种因果表示自编码器，通过"导数式"分词（细节帧仅用少量 token 表示相对锚帧的增量）突破视频生成器受限于数秒片段的瓶颈，使一分钟长视频的分词与生成成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间视频生成器只在数秒片段上训练，瓶颈主要来自分词器：(1) 对静态帧与运动帧分配相同数量的 token，存在冗余；(2) 生成时需要未来帧，而非因果的分词器在生成阶段无法获得这些未来帧。因此需要一种因果、token 高效的视频分词方案。

Method: 提出 DeltaRAE：其分词采用"导数式"策略，周期性锚帧（anchor frames）被完整编码，其余帧每帧仅用少量 token 编码其相对变化；编码器与解码器均为因果结构，并读取一个冻结的预训练图像编码器作为表示源。

Result: 在 UCF-101 和 Kinetics-600 上，基于 DeltaRAE 潜变量训练的标准扩散 Transformer 与当前最优视频分词器性能相当。相比对每帧保持相同 patch token 数的方案，DeltaRAE 在 16 帧片段上使分词器 GFLOPs 减半，并以 18.8 倍更少的 token 表示一分钟视频。分词器可在一分钟视频上端到端训练；在视频预测任务中，基于其潜变量的生成器能生成完整一分钟视频。对照实验显示，在 token 预算相同但无预训练编码器时，同一生成器在多数视频中 15 秒内即崩溃。

Conclusion: DeltaRAE 通过因果、导数式分词与冻结预训练图像编码器的结合，显著提升 token 与计算效率，将视频生成的有效时长从数秒扩展到一分钟，并表明预训练编码器表示对长时生成稳定性不可或缺。

Abstract: Latent video generators are trained on clips of a few seconds, and much of this limit lies in their tokenizers, which spend as many tokens on a static frame as on a moving one and need future frames that do not exist during generation. We introduce DeltaRAE, a causal representation autoencoder for video. Its derivative tokenization encodes periodic anchor frames in full and all remaining frames with a few tokens each, and its causal encoder and decoder read a frozen pretrained image encoder. On UCF-101 and Kinetics-600, a standard diffusion transformer trained on DeltaRAE latents is competitive with state-of-the-art video tokenizers. DeltaRAE halves the tokenizer GFLOPs on 16-frame clips relative to keeping the same number of patch tokens for every frame, and it represents a minute of video with 18.8x fewer tokens. The tokenizer trains end to end on one-minute videos, and in video prediction a generator on its latents produces a full minute of video. The same generator collapses within 15 seconds in most videos when the tokenizer has the same token budget but no pretrained encoder.

</details>


### [96] [SIA: Selective Image Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33894)
*Amirhossein Alimohammadi,Kian Izadpanah,Bardiya Kariminia,Yiorgos Chrysanthou,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Selective Image Analogy（SIA）新设定：单对演示图像编码多种编辑，推理时用户可选择性应用子集；构建多编辑类比数据集，并用 mask 对齐抑制机制（token-gated LoRA + 概念抑制向量）实现可控编辑迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像类比方法假设 (A, A') 中的所有变化都必须被完整迁移到查询图像 B，缺乏灵活性；用户往往只想应用演示编辑中的一部分，而现有方法需要为每种编辑组合重新提供演示对，代价高。

Method: 1) 定义 SIA 任务：单一演示对编码多重编辑，推理时选择性地将部分编辑迁移到 B；2) 构建多编辑类比数据集，包含 full-edit 与 partial-edit 两种目标；3) 训练分两阶段：先学习迁移完整的演示变换，再通过 mask 对齐抑制机制获得选择控制——包括 token-gated LoRA（在去噪过程中将 adapter 更新限制在被抑制的编辑 token 上）与 concept suppression vector（在去噪前削弱文本嵌入中选中的 token）。

Result: 定量与定性实验及用户研究均验证了方法有效性：能够抑制 A' 中仍然可见但用户未选择的多余编辑，同时保留所选编辑，且无需为每种编辑组合重新提供演示对。

Conclusion: SIA 将图像类比从"全量迁移"拓展为"选择性迁移"，通过 token 级抑制机制在单对演示下实现多编辑的灵活组合控制，为可控图像编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image analogy transfers a demonstrated transformation from a before/after pair (A, A') to a new query image B. Existing methods assume all changes in (A, A') should be fully transferred, limiting flexibility when users want only a subset of the demonstrated edits. We introduce Selective Image Analogy (SIA), a new setting where a single demonstration pair encodes multiple edits, and the user selects at inference time which edits to apply to B. To support this, we curate a multi-edit analogy dataset with full-edit and partial-edit targets. We then train a model that first learns to transfer the complete demonstrated transformation, and then gains selective control through a mask-aligned suppression mechanism: a token-gated LoRA that restricts adapter updates to suppressed edit tokens during denoising, paired with a concept suppression vector that weakens the selected tokens in the text embeddings before denoising. Together, these components allow SIA to suppress unwanted edits that remain visible in A', while preserving the user-selected ones, all without requiring a new demonstration pair for each edit combination. We perform extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations along with user studies to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [97] [TWIST: Teacher-Weighted Initial-Noise Spherical Transform for Quality-Preserving Diversity in Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33895)
*Shayan Baghayi-Nejad,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWIST：将推理时的初始噪声优化摊销为一个轻量网络，用教师引导的DPP目标转换初始噪声低频分量，在少步扩散模型中低成本提升多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时刻蒸馏扩散模型（如SDXL-Turbo/Lightning）能少步生成高质量图像，但模式覆盖与多样性下降。已有方法在推理时优化初始噪声以提升多样性，需对去噪器反向传播，成本高；且其多样性目标通常只是让样本相互排斥，未考虑教师模型输出分布。

Method: 提出Teacher-Weighted Initial-noise Spherical Transform（TWIST）：一个轻量网络，仅变换初始噪声的低频分量以提升多样性，无需推理时优化。采用教师引导的行列式点过程（DPP）目标，使学生样本在特征空间的多样性与教师样本对齐；把噪声优化摊销进单一网络。

Result: 在SDXL-Turbo上，TWIST以不到MoNO 25%的推理成本达到其多样性，并在同等计算预算下取得更高recall及更好的PickScore、ImageReward和CLIPScore。在SDXL-Lightning上，以更低成本取得相当recall，且无MoNO的ImageReward退化。

Conclusion: 将初始噪声多样性优化摊销为单次网络前向，可在保持少步生成效率的同时恢复多样性，并以教师分布为参照避免盲目排斥式多样性目标。

Abstract: Timestep-distilled diffusion models generate high-quality images in one or a few steps, but often suffer from reduced mode coverage and limited diversity. Recent methods improve diversity by optimizing the initial noise at inference time, requiring costly backpropagation through the denoiser. Moreover, their diversity objectives typically repel generated samples without accounting for how the corresponding teacher model distributes its outputs. We introduce Teacher-Weighted Initial-noise Spherical Transform (TWIST), a lightweight network that transforms the low-frequency components of initial noise to improve diversity without inference-time optimization. TWIST uses a teacher-guided determinantal point process (DPP) objective to align the feature-space diversity of student samples with that of the teacher. By amortizing noise optimization into a single network, TWIST eliminates inference-time backpropagation while preserving the efficiency of few-step generation. On SDXL-Turbo, TWIST matches the diversity of MoNO at less than 25% of its inference cost, while achieving higher recall and better PickScore, ImageReward, and CLIPScore under matched compute budgets. On SDXL-Lightning, it achieves comparable recall at lower cost without the ImageReward degradation observed with MoNO.

</details>


### [98] [RelPRM: Grounding Visual Process Reward Models in Relational Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33903)
*Zhaoxing Zhang,Ziqiu Gui,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向视觉关系的过程奖励建模方法RelPRM，并构建自动化验证管线及数据集RelProcessTrain/RelProcessBench，显著提升视觉过程奖励模型对关系性错误的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉过程奖励模型（PRM）能检出计算错误，却会对"误读视觉关系"的推理步骤给出高分；而基于后续答案成功与否的监督无法直接判定某一步的关系性断言是否成立，故需强化视觉关系验证。

Method: 首先构建自动化管线，程序化验证每条断言并定位所需对象、关系与图像区域，产出训练集RelProcessTrain与基准RelProcessBench（RPB），通过受控图像编辑与步骤改写检验评分是否对关系证据敏感。在此基础上提出RelPRM，含两种训练机制：(1)关系证据自蒸馏，构造保留必要对象、关系与依赖的视图，降低复杂场景中寻找决定性证据的负担，并将证据辅助判断迁移到全图；(2)关系证据驱动的一致性训练，鼓励关系编辑反转步骤真值时评分相应变化、无关编辑下保持稳定、改写间评分变化一致。推理时仅用原图与推理上下文直接对当前步打分。

Result: RelPRM在VisualProcessBench（VPB）上达66.2%，在RPB上达68.4%，分别较VisualPRM-8B提升4.2与9.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 针对视觉关系进行训练可同时提升关系验证能力与步骤评估质量，并泛化到更广泛的多模态推理任务。

Abstract: Visual process reward models (PRMs) can detect calculation errors yet assign high scores to steps that misread visual relations. Supervision based on subsequent answer success does not directly establish whether a step's relational claim holds. To strengthen visual-relation verification, we first build an automated pipeline that programmatically verifies each claim and identifies the required objects, relations, and image regions. It produces RelProcessTrain and RelProcessBench (RPB), which tests whether scores respond appropriately to relational evidence through controlled image edits and step rephrasings. Building on this pipeline, we further introduce RelPRM, a visual-relation-oriented method for process reward modeling with two training mechanisms. Relation-evidence self-distillation constructs views preserving necessary objects, relations, and dependencies, reducing the burden of finding decisive evidence in complex scenes and transferring reliable evidence-assisted judgments to full images. Relation-evidence-driven consistency training encourages appropriate score changes when relation edits reverse a step's truth, stability under irrelevant edits, and similar score changes across paraphrases. At inference, RelPRM directly scores the current step using only the original image and reasoning context. RelPRM achieves 66.2% on VisualProcessBench (VPB) and 68.4% on RPB, gains of 4.2 and 9.9 percentage points over VisualPRM-8B. These results show that training for visual relations improves both relational verification and step assessment across broader multimodal reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [99] [Tortho-Gaussian++: Suppressing Geometric Drift and Floaters for Robust Gravity-Aligned TDOM Generation in Large-Scale Aerial Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33905)
*Xiang Wang,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tortho-Gaussian++ 是一个面向 TDOM 生成的 3DGS 训练框架，通过几何可观测性分析剪枝外围弱支撑高斯、改进致密化时优化器状态初始化以抑制漂移与漂浮物，并结合图像重力线索、相机分布先验与天底视图实现稳健重力对齐正交投影渲染；在多个大规模 UAV 数据集上平均 PSNR 提升 0.5–3.5 dB，训练时间、显存与高斯数量分别降低 45.5%、47.2%、58.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在空中场景重建中表现优异，并被扩展用于真实数字正射影像图（TDOM）生成，但存在几何漂移、漂浮物（floaters）以及外围区域几何支撑不足导致渲染不可靠的问题；同时 TDOM 渲染需要稳健的重力对齐正交投影参考，而现有方法往往依赖外部先验或对倾斜敏感。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 基于视角射线的几何可观测性分析，定量刻画每个高斯点的几何支撑可靠性，并剪枝支撑特别弱的外围区域；(2) 改进致密化过程中新建高斯的优化器状态初始化，在无外部先验条件下从机制上抑制漂移与漂浮物；(3) 融合图像导出的重力线索、相机分布先验与天底视图，稳健估计重力方向，为 TDOM 渲染提供重力对齐的正交投影参考。

Result: 在多个大规模 UAV 数据集上，相比原始 3DGS 平均 PSNR 提升 0.5–3.5 dB，性能与先验引导的 3DGS 方法相当或更优；训练时间、显存占用和高斯数量分别减少 45.5%、47.2% 和 58.6%。定性评估显示方法有效抑制漂移与漂浮物，稳健重力方向估计减少了 TDOM 中倾斜引起的立面伪影。

Conclusion: Tortho-Gaussian++ 在无需外部先验的前提下，同时提升了 TDOM 生成的几何一致性、渲染质量与计算效率，是一种高效且鲁棒的大规模 UAV 场景正射影像生成方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable performance in aerial scene reconstruction, motivating its recent extension to true digital orthophoto maps (TDOMs) generation. We propose Tortho-Gaussian++, a novel 3DGS training framework that suppresses geometric drift and floaters and automatically prunes unreliable peripheral regions, coupled with a robust gravity-aligned TDOM rendering solution. First, we explore geometric support via viewing-ray-based geometric observability analysis, quantitatively characterizing the reliability for each Gaussian and subsequently pruning peripheral regions with particularly weak geometric support. Second, the optimizer state initialization is refined for newly created Gaussians during densification, intrinsically suppressing drift and floaters without external priors. Lastly, a robust gravity direction estimation solution is designed, combining image-derived gravity cues, camera-distribution priors and nadir views, to achieve a gravity-aligned orthogonal projection reference for TDOM rendering. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale UAV (Unmanned Aerial Vehicle) datasets demonstrate that our Tortho-Gaussian++ outperforms vanilla 3DGS by 0.5-3.5 dB in average PSNR and yields comparable or superior performance to prior-guided 3DGS methods. Notably, it reduces the training time, VRAM and Gaussian number by 45.5%, 47.2%, and 58.6%, respectively. Qualitative evaluations confirm that our method effectively suppresses drift and floaters, while robust gravity direction estimation reduces tilt-induced facade artifacts in TDOMs.

</details>


### [100] [Event-WAM: Learning to Act with Event-Derived Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33917)
*Jongseong Bae,Hyunwoo Kim,Yeongin Lee,Eubin So,Ha Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Event-WAM 将 RGB 演示中导出的事件（event）视觉变化作为辅助未来监督，与 RGB 联合进行潜空间流匹配训练，推理时无需事件观测或显式未来生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过未来视觉建模学习机器人动作，但现有研究多依赖传统 RGB 预测目标。作者希望引入 RGB 之外的互补监督信号（视觉变化），以提升动作学习效果与扰动鲁棒性。

Method: 从 RGB 演示构建时间结构化的事件表示，直接用冻结的预训练 RGB 视频 VAE 编码（不训练事件专用 VAE）；采用 RGB–Event 联合潜空间流匹配，同时提供外观导向与变化导向的监督；推理阶段不需要事件观测，也不生成未来帧。

Result: 仿真基准上标准条件性能相当，扰动下鲁棒性提升具基准依赖性（如 LIBERO-Plus 上有改进）；真实世界实验在标准与未见扰动条件下操作成功率均提升。进一步分析显示策略提升并不总是伴随更高的未来预测保真度。

Conclusion: 变化导向的监督对动作学习具有潜在价值，可作为 RGB 未来预测的替代或互补目标；策略性能与未来预测保真度并不必然一致。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage future visual modeling for robotic action learning, with recent studies exploring alternative or complementary objectives beyond conventional RGB prediction. We introduce Event-WAM, which incorporates event-derived visual changes as auxiliary future supervision alongside RGB. Event-WAM constructs temporally structured event representations from RGB demonstrations and encodes them using a frozen pretrained RGB video VAE, without training an event-specific VAE. Joint RGB--Event latent flow matching provides appearance- and change-oriented supervision without requiring event observations or explicit future generation at inference. Across simulated benchmarks, Event-WAM maintains comparable performance under standard conditions, while robustness gains under perturbations are benchmark-dependent, including improvements on LIBERO-Plus. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate improved manipulation success under both standard and unseen perturbation conditions. Further analyses show that policy improvements are not consistently accompanied by higher future prediction fidelity, suggesting the potential of change-oriented supervision for action learning.

</details>


### [101] [UPP-Gauss: Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting for B-mode Novel-View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33920)
*Guil Jung,Seok-Hwan Oh,Myeong-Gee Kim,Young-Min Kim,Hyeon-Jik Lee,Sang-Yun Kim,Hyeok-Jun Kwon,Hyuk-Sool Kwon,Hyeon-Min Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UPP-Gauss：将超声声学传播物理引入显式3D高斯表示，用探头位姿与声束方向采样三个高斯场构建声学图并合成B-mode图像，实现高保真、高效的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-mode超声新视角合成需同时建模组织中的声传播过程与随探头位姿变化的外观差异，现有方法难以准确刻画这些声学交互。

Method: 提出Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting：显式3D高斯表示中融入声学交互；依据探头位姿与声束传播方向采样三个3D高斯场生成声学图，再按超声传播规律组合这些图以合成B-mode图像。

Result: 在三个声学特性各异的数据集上取得最佳重建质量与高效渲染，相比各数据集最优基线PSNR平均提升1.43 dB。

Conclusion: 通过对超声传播的显式高斯建模，可实现准确且高效的B-mode新视角合成。

Abstract: Accurate novel-view synthesis of B-mode ultrasound images requires modeling acoustic propagation through tissue and the resulting appearance variations across probe poses. We propose Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting (UPP-Gauss), which integrates these acoustic interactions into an explicit Gaussian representation. UPP-Gauss constructs acoustic maps by sampling three 3D Gaussian fields using the probe pose and acoustic beam propagation direction, then combines these maps according to ultrasound propagation to synthesize B-mode images. Across three acoustically diverse datasets, UPP-Gauss achieves the best reconstruction quality with efficient rendering, improving PSNR over the best baseline on each dataset by 1.43 dB on average. These results demonstrate accurate and efficient B-mode novel-view synthesis through explicit Gaussian modeling of ultrasound propagation.

</details>


### [102] [Towards 360$^\circ$ Embodied UAV Tracking Without Turning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33934)
*Bingyu Li,Wu Du,Zhiyuan Zhao,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景感知下的无人机目标跟踪新范式与基准360UAV-ETrack，并给出全景VLA框架360UAV-TVLA，在两种VLA骨干上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统具身无人机跟踪将目标可观测性与航向控制耦合：前视相机易使目标脱离视野。作者研究无需主动调整航向、仅凭全周球面感知跟随指定目标的互补范式，并指出该设定缺乏基准与方法支撑。

Method: 1) 基准：360UAV-ETrack，含2,652条序列、超173万条同步观测、8类跟踪任务、150个留出闭环场景；2) 方法：360UAV-TVLA全景视觉—语言—动作框架，由持续视觉指代(PVR)、压缩时序记忆(CTM)、几何对齐轨迹监督(GTS)组成，分别用于实例识别、运动推理与三维控制。

Result: 在两个VLA骨干上一致优于标准微调：π0归一化平均跟踪帧数由22.56%升至35.28%，π0.5由25.68%升至33.64%；同时揭示离线模仿精度与闭环跟踪可靠性之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 全景感知+不调整航向的跟踪范式可行且有效，360UAV-ETrack可作为兼具挑战性的测试平台，离线精度不足以代表闭环可靠性。

Abstract: Conventional embodied UAV tracking couples target observability with heading control because targets may leave the field of view of forward-facing cameras. We investigate a complementary paradigm that uses full-spherical perception to follow designated targets without active heading adjustment. To support this setting, we introduce 360UAV-ETrack, a simulation benchmark comprising 2,652 sequences, over 1.73 million synchronized observations, eight tracking tasks, and 150 held-out closed-loop scenarios. We propose 360UAV-TVLA, a panoramic vision--language--action (VLA) framework integrating persistent visual referring (PVR), compressed temporal memory (CTM), and geometry-aligned trajectory supervision (GTS) for instance identification, motion reasoning, and three-dimensional control. Experiments across two VLA backbones show consistent gains over standard fine-tuning, with normalized average tracking frames increasing from 22.56\% to 35.28\% for $π_0$ and from 25.68\% to 33.64\% for $π_{0.5}$. Our benchmark further reveals a substantial gap between offline imitation accuracy and closed-loop tracking reliability, establishing a challenging testbed for future research https://libingyu01.github.io/360UAV-ETrack_360UAV-TVLA/

</details>


### [103] [BayesPath: Reliability-Aware Bayesian Reasoning for Whole-Slide Pathology Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33935)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Jongwoo Kim,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BayesPath 是一个免训练的全切片图像（WSI）智能体，将病理学家的"扫描-观察-权衡-决策"过程建模为可靠性感知的贝叶斯推理，在五个 MultiPathQA 任务上取得 58.6% 的平均准确率，显著优于现有免训练智能体（34.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练 WSI 智能体缺乏显式推理框架：仅用 LLM 聚合 MLLM 的区域描述，既没有对候选诊断的信念分布，也不考虑描述可靠性，导致模型的重复错误被误当作独立证据，从而强化错误诊断。

Method: 提出 BayesPath，将显式的候选信念分布随每次观测通过可靠性感知似然进行更新；该似然校正模型的答案偏置、对相关观测降权，并在无标签条件下估计诊断混淆。信念分布同时指导下一步观察位置与停止时机，更新过程形成可解释的证据链。

Result: 在覆盖切片分类与切片 VQA 的五个 MultiPathQA 任务上，BayesPath 平均准确率 58.6%，次优方法为 34.1%，切片分类提升尤为显著；在相同观测下贝叶斯更新优于（哪怕是更大 LLM 的）LLM 聚合；信念校准优于朴素贝叶斯，可将不确定病例转交病理学家。

Conclusion: 将 WSI 诊断推理形式化为可靠性感知的贝叶斯更新，可有效避免错误证据累积、提升准确率与校准性，并为不确定病例的人机协作提供可靠依据。

Abstract: Diagnosing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires hypothesis-driven reasoning: pathologists form a differential diagnosis, seek discriminative evidence, weigh its reliability, and stop once it suffices. However, existing training-free WSI agents lack an explicit framework for such reasoning. They typically use a large language model (LLM) to aggregate the region descriptions of a multimodal LLM (MLLM), without a belief over the candidates or regard for the descriptions' reliability. Consequently, a repeated model error counts as independent evidence for an incorrect diagnosis. We introduce BayesPath, a training-free WSI agent that casts the pathologist's Scan-Look-Weigh-Decide process as reliability-aware Bayesian reasoning. An explicit belief over the candidates is updated with each observation through a reliability-aware likelihood and guides where to look next and when to stop. This likelihood corrects for the models' answer bias, discounts correlated observations, and captures diagnostic confusions estimated without labels. The updates form an interpretable evidence trail of how each observation shapes the belief. On five MultiPathQA tasks spanning slide classification and slide VQA, BayesPath achieves the highest average accuracy among training-free agents, 58.6% against 34.1% for the next best, with large gains on slide classification. On identical observations, Bayesian updating outperforms LLM aggregation on average, even with a larger LLM. BayesPath's belief is also better calibrated than naive Bayes, so uncertain cases can be deferred to pathologists. Code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BayesPath

</details>


### [104] [TAT-Det: A Dataset for Testicular Appendage Torsion Detection and Segmentation in Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33940)
*Ying Jiang,Juntao Jiang,Linxuan Fan,Lingling Li,Yaqi Wang,Xiuzhen Yang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Jingjing Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布TAT-Det——首个面向儿童睾丸附件扭转(TAT)的专家标注超声数据集，含1,203例患者的2,600张图像，提供病变级检测框与像素级分割掩码，并给出目标检测与语义分割基线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAT是青春期前儿童急性阴囊疼痛的首要病因，但临床表现非特异，且缺乏标准化的儿童急性阴囊急症数据集，导致诊断困难、AI研究缺乏数据基础。

Method: 在浙江大学医学院附属儿童医院采集1,203例患者的2,600张超声图像；每张TAT阳性图像标注病变级边界框（检测）与像素级分割掩码（精细描绘）；标注采用多阶段流程：由认证儿科超声医师独立标注、共识复核与精修；并提供代表性深度学习模型的检测与分割基线。

Result: YOLO11m取得最佳检测性能，RWKV-UNet取得最佳分割性能。

Conclusion: TAT-Det弥补了儿童急诊超声公开资源缺失的空白，为自动化TAT分析的AI方法开发与评估奠定基础。
